The text batch B16

Written by node tools/text-render.mjs --batch (E66 C6) from docs/dev/text-batch-B01a.json. n = 1, 2, 5 where the text counts (a family through Intl.PluralRules); #1, #2, #3 fill a person with Aiko, then Daichi (a woman's name, a man's), a room with Warsztat, Biblioteka, Kino and an item with Nóż kuchenny, Lina, Krzesło (masculine, feminine, neuter), each in turn. The facts are numbered from 0, as `kept` names them. HTML in a string is shown as written.

The texts (418)

1. DRPG.Investigation.finalAskTitle

window · window-title · investigation · gm

Facts (kept in bold):

  1. The window asks whether to place the Final Truth Remnant whose room the GM picked on the dashboard's form, when the dashboard closed without Save; its default button is Place it and the other is Not now. (scripts/investigation.mjs offerFinalOnClose)
EN before
Place the Final Truth Remnant?
EN after
Place the Final Truth Remnant
PL przed
Postawić Final Truth Remnant?
PL po
Postaw Final Truth Remnant

Removed, and where it went:

  • The question mark: a window title is the window's subject, never a sentence (section 3, the surface matrix's window-title row); the question stays in the body, DRPG.Investigation.finalAskBody.
  • PL: the question goes from the title (surface matrix, window-title row); the title takes the imperative shape of the module's other titles (Usuń Remnant, Postaw ten Key Remnant).
  • EN review: nothing left open
  • cold EN review: nothing left open
  • PL review: nothing left open
  • mechanics review: nothing left open

2. DRPG.Investigation.finalAskBody

window · gm-diagnostics · investigation · gm

Facts (kept in bold):

  1. Asked when the dashboard closes by anything but Save (the X, Escape, Close or one of the footer's other buttons) while the Final Truth form has a room picked; the window's text is written on that close, the token is not placed unasked. (scripts/investigation.mjs offerFinalOnClose)
  2. Place it places the Final Truth Remnant as Save places it (placeFinalFromDashboard); Not now places nothing. (scripts/investigation.mjs offerFinalOnClose)
EN before
A room is picked for the Final Truth Remnant, but the window was closed without Save. Place it now?
EN after
You picked a room for the Final Truth Remnant and closed the window without saving. Place it now?
PL przed
Dla Final Truth Remnant wybrano pokój, ale okno zamknięto bez Zapisz. Postawić go teraz?
PL po
Final Truth Remnant ma już wybrany pokój, ale zamykasz okno, nie klikając Zapisz. Postawić go teraz?

Removed, and where it went:

  • "A room is picked ... the window was closed": the GM is the one who picked and closed, so the text says so (X11, V1).
  • PL: the impersonal "wybrano ... zamknięto" hid the GM who picked and closed (X11, V1); the present and the adverbial participle say it with no gender (PL17).
  • REV: coldEn: "without Save" becomes "without saving".
  • EN review: nothing left open
  • cold EN review: nothing left open
  • PL review: nothing left open
  • mechanics review: nothing left open

19. DRPG.Investigation.neverOnePerson

window · gm-diagnostics · investigation · gm

Facts (kept in bold):

  1. The rule line over the Remnants table, drawn on every view of the tab. (scripts/investigation.mjs caseTracesPanel)
  2. A Key Remnant narrows the suspects to at most half the cast and any other to two to four: a rule for the GM writing the words, which no function checks. (not found in code: the line is guidance for what the GM writes on a Remnant; KEY_REMNANTS.suspectRange (2, 4) is the plan's range for the clues together, not a check on one Remnant)
EN before
A trace never names anyone: a Key Remnant narrows it to at most half the cast, any other to 2-4.
EN after
A Remnant names nobody: a Key Remnant narrows the suspects to at most half the cast, any other to 2-4.
PL przed
Ślad nigdy nie wskazuje nazwiska: Key Remnant zawęża krąg najwyżej do połowy obsady, inny do 2-4 osób.
PL po
Remnant nigdy nie wskazuje nikogo wprost: Key Remnant zawęża krąg podejrzanych najwyżej do połowy obsady, każdy inny do 2-4 osób.

Removed, and where it went:

  • "A trace" becomes "A Remnant" (EN15, the glossary's remnant), and "narrows it" becomes "narrows the suspects", since "it" had no noun to stand for (PL23).
  • PL: "Ślad" becomes "Remnant" (PL-r, the glossary's remnant); "nie wskazuje nazwiska" becomes "nie wskazuje nikogo wprost", and the circle is the suspects' (PL23).
  • FIX: R3970: "never names anyone" becomes "names nobody": "the suspects" made the hint 21 words, and the Investigation's standing hints hold at most 20 (the owner's text rule of 12.11).

Needs code:

  • DRPG.Investigation.neverOnePerson: "2-4" (both languages) is KEY_REMNANTS.suspectRange typed into the prose (O5, M5); it would need placeholders filled in caseTracesPanel, as plannerIntro fills {min}-{max}.
  • EN review: nothing left open
  • cold EN review: nothing left open
  • PL review: nothing left open
  • mechanics review: nothing left open

43. DRPG.Investigation.tooThin

window · gm-diagnostics · investigation · gm

Facts (kept in bold):

  1. Shown in a warning box when the chapter's found Key Remnants (the plan's found rows and the off-plan finds) are fewer than KEY_REMNANTS.minimum + 1, so it also shows at exactly the minimum. (scripts/investigation.mjs caseKeyPanel)
  2. {min} is KEY_REMNANTS.minimum; a plural family on the found count, its own sentence for none. (scripts/investigation.mjs tooThinLine)
  3. The unfound Key Remnants are charged in Despair to each Monokuma's pool when the Class Trial opens, not at the vote. (scripts/investigation.mjs chargeForUnfoundKeys)
  4. The old text says the GM pays for it at the vote; the code charges as the Class Trial opens. (scripts/investigation.mjs chargeForUnfoundKeys)
EN before
n=1 (one) Only 1 Key Remnant has been found. The minimum is 1 - below that the trial has no honest answer. Point the players at what is left, or let it stand and pay for it at the vote.
n=2 (other) Only 2 Key Remnants have been found. The minimum is 2 - below that the trial has no honest answer. Point the players at what is left, or let it stand and pay for it at the vote.
n=5 (other) Only 5 Key Remnants have been found. The minimum is 5 - below that the trial has no honest answer. Point the players at what is left, or let it stand and pay for it at the vote.
EN after
n=1 (one) Only 1 Key Remnant has been found, and the trial needs more than 1 to have an honest answer. Point the players at what is left, or let it stand: each Monokuma's pool gains Despair when the Class Trial opens.
n=2 (other) Only 2 Key Remnants have been found, and the trial needs more than 2 to have an honest answer. Point the players at what is left, or let it stand: each Monokuma's pool gains Despair when the Class Trial opens.
n=5 (other) Only 5 Key Remnants have been found, and the trial needs more than 5 to have an honest answer. Point the players at what is left, or let it stand: each Monokuma's pool gains Despair when the Class Trial opens.
PL przed
n=1 (one) Znaleziono tylko 1 Key Remnant. Minimum to 1 - poniżej Class Trial nie ma uczciwej odpowiedzi. Skieruj graczy na resztę albo zostaw to i zapłać przy głosowaniu.
n=2 (few) Znaleziono tylko 2 Key Remnanty. Minimum to 2 - poniżej Class Trial nie ma uczciwej odpowiedzi. Skieruj graczy na resztę albo zostaw to i zapłać przy głosowaniu.
n=5 (many) Znaleziono tylko 5 Key Remnantów. Minimum to 5 - poniżej Class Trial nie ma uczciwej odpowiedzi. Skieruj graczy na resztę albo zostaw to i zapłać przy głosowaniu.
PL po
n=1 (one) Znaleziono dopiero 1 Key Remnant, a żeby Class Trial miał uczciwe rozwiązanie, trzeba więcej niż 1. Naprowadź graczy na to, czego jeszcze nie znaleźli, albo zostaw to tak, jak jest: na początku Class Trial każdy Monokuma dostanie do puli Despair za nieznalezione Key Remnanty.
n=2 (few) Znaleziono dopiero 2 Key Remnanty, a żeby Class Trial miał uczciwe rozwiązanie, trzeba więcej niż 2. Naprowadź graczy na to, czego jeszcze nie znaleźli, albo zostaw to tak, jak jest: na początku Class Trial każdy Monokuma dostanie do puli Despair za nieznalezione Key Remnanty.
n=5 (many) Znaleziono dopiero 5 Key Remnantów, a żeby Class Trial miał uczciwe rozwiązanie, trzeba więcej niż 5. Naprowadź graczy na to, czego jeszcze nie znaleźli, albo zostaw to tak, jak jest: na początku Class Trial każdy Monokuma dostanie do puli Despair za nieznalezione Key Remnanty.

Removed, and where it went:

  • The dash aside " - below that the trial has no honest answer" joins the first sentence (X4); "The minimum is {min}" becomes "below {min}", keeping the number and its meaning (M4).
  • "pay for it at the vote" becomes "pay for it when the Class Trial opens": the charge is made as the trial opens (M2, facts 2 and 3).
  • PL: the dash aside joins the first sentence (X4); "Minimum to {min}" becomes "trzeba więcej niż {min}", since the box shows at {min} too (M4, fact 0); "zapłać przy głosowaniu" becomes the Despair each Monokuma's pool takes as the Class Trial opens (M2, facts 2 and 3; PL4); "Skieruj graczy na resztę" becomes "Naprowadź graczy na to, czego jeszcze nie znaleźli" (step 4).
  • REV: en: "below {min}" becomes "the trial needs more than {min} to have an honest answer": the box shows at exactly {min} too (fact 0), so the reviewer's "the minimum is {min}; below it" would still read as nothing wrong at {min}; the Polish's "trzeba więcej niż {min}" says the same (M4).
  • REV: en: "let it stand and pay for it when the Class Trial opens" becomes "let it stand: each Monokuma's pool gains Despair when the Class Trial opens": who pays and in what (fact 2; the cold reader's point too).
  • REV: pl: „zostaw to tak:” becomes „zostaw to tak, jak jest:”, which reads whole aloud (step 1).
  • EN review: nothing left open
  • cold EN review: nothing left open
  • PL review: nothing left open
  • mechanics review: nothing left open

PL po
n=1 (one) Gracz wskazał punkt zainteresowania, a ty uznajesz, że coś tam jest. To okno stawia Key Remnant na mapie i może go podpiąć pod 1 zaplanowany trop, jeśli nie ma go jeszcze na mapie.
n=2 (few) Gracz wskazał punkt zainteresowania, a ty uznajesz, że coś tam jest. To okno stawia Key Remnant na mapie i może go podpiąć pod jeden z 2 zaplanowanych tropów, którego nie ma jeszcze na mapie.
n=5 (many) Gracz wskazał punkt zainteresowania, a ty uznajesz, że coś tam jest. To okno stawia Key Remnant na mapie i może go podpiąć pod jeden z 5 zaplanowanych tropów, którego nie ma jeszcze na mapie.

  • PL: "Wskazali" had no one before it (X13, P1, PL23): "Gracz wskazał"; "To stawia Remnant" becomes "To okno stawia Key Remnant", what the window places (O4).

  • REV: coldEn: "hang it on" becomes "attach it to".

Disagrees with the code or a decision:

  • DRPG.Investigation.createHereIntro: {n} is plan.entries.length, every planned row, while the Which planned clue list offers only the rows not yet on the map, so the line can say "one of the 5 clues" with two offered. The owner decides whether {n} should count the open rows.
  • EN review: nothing left open
  • cold EN review: unnatural
    • open: "attach" taken; the one form keeps "the {n} clue", since the Polish one form and the family carry {n} and dropping it from one form is a code-side choice (N2), not a wording one. The one form renders "hang it on the 1 clue you planned": the numeral is odd with "the", and "hang it on" is slang for attach. Proposed one: "... This puts a Key Remnant on the map and can attach it to the clue you planned." other: "... and can attach it to one of the {n} clues you planned."
  • PL review: nothing left open
  • mechanics review: nothing left open

n=5 something the code puts here: 5 changed, 5 skipped as older than the last reset
PL przed
n=1 coś, co wstawia kod: zmienione 1, pominięte pod cięciem resetu 1
n=2 coś, co wstawia kod: zmienione 2, pominięte pod cięciem resetu 2
n=5 coś, co wstawia kod: zmienione 5, pominięte pod cięciem resetu 5
PL po
n=1 coś, co wstawia kod: zmienione 1, pominięte sprzed resetu 1
n=2 coś, co wstawia kod: zmienione 2, pominięte sprzed resetu 2
n=5 coś, co wstawia kod: zmienione 5, pominięte sprzed resetu 5

Removed, and where it went:

  • unchanged: a list line in the toast after DRPG.Case.restored, label and counts (section 14); no number typed (M5).
  • PL: „pominięte pod cięciem resetu” becomes „pominięte sprzed resetu”: a calque of the reset's cut; the rows left out are those at or before the reset (fact 1; step 6).
  • REV: coldEn: "under the reset's cut left out" becomes "left out as older than the last reset"; "season" in the proposal was not taken: a reset cuts a group of stores (gm-stores.mjs resetGroup).
  • FIX: R1157: "{cut} left out" becomes "{cut} skipped": a count before "left" reads as a flat counter to the census of counts (E66-15); the same fact, no family needed.

Needs code:

  • {n} and {cut} are counts given to game.i18n.format, not plural() families, so "1 changed" and "1 ... left out" read without a noun; a plural family would need a code change in gm-stores.mjs restoreCase.
  • EN review: nothing left open
  • cold EN review: nothing left open
  • PL review: nothing left open
  • mechanics review: nothing left open

A GM decides each, and game.drpg.migrateRemnants() names them.
n=5 (other) 5 Remnants on the map (of 5) keep flags of their answer keys on their tokens, which every browser holds. Each one's flags are left by a Faint Prep promotion that a later correction overrode, have no ledger row to move into, or were not stripped. A GM decides each, and game.drpg.migrateRemnants() names them.
PL przed

n=5 (many) 5 Remnantów na mapie (z 5) zachowuje na swoich żetonach, które ma każda przeglądarka, flagi kluczy odpowiedzi - awans Faint Prep, przeciw któremu stanęła późniejsza poprawka, albo flagi bez wiersza w rejestrze, do którego by je przeniesiono: rozstrzyga o każdym GM, a game.drpg.migrateRemnants() je wskazuje.

n=2 (few) 2 Remnanty na mapie (z 2) zachowują na swoich tokenach, które ma każda przeglądarka, flagi kluczy odpowiedzi.   • DRPG.Case.chargeHeld says "on the primary GM's browser", the usual case; a GM who runs the charge from a console is counted on their own browser (fact 1). The owner decides whether the text should cover that road.

EN before

EN after

#2 Remove Daichi from the case? A node that rests on it keeps naming it, and Check consistency reports it.
PL przed
#1 Usunąć Aiko ze sprawy? Węzeł, który na nim stoi, nadal go wskazuje, a sprawdzenie to pokaże.
#2 Usunąć Daichi ze sprawy? Węzeł, który na nim stoi, nadal go wskazuje, a sprawdzenie to pokaże.
PL po
#1 Usunąć węzeł „Aiko” ze sprawy? Węzły, które się do niego odwołują, nadal będą go wskazywać, a sprawdzenie to pokaże.
#2 Usunąć węzeł „Daichi” ze sprawy? Węzły, które się do niego odwołują, nadal będą go wskazywać, a sprawdzenie to pokaże.

  • PL: "który na nim stoi" mirrored "rests on"; the form's own label for that field is "Wynika z" (step 6, T22). "nim"/"go" assumed a masculine {name}; the name is quoted and the pronouns are neuter, true for any node's name (step 5, PL-g).
  • REV: pl: „węzeł” names what is removed, so „go” has a noun; „które z tego wynikają” becomes „które się do niego odwołują”, inputs and targets both (fact 1; steps 2-3).
  • REV: coldEn: "the check says so" names the button, Check consistency (fact 1).
  • EN review: nothing left open
  • cold EN review: nothing left open
  • PL review: nothing left open
  • mechanics review: nothing left open

  3. The canvas places only nodes that have a position. (scripts/case-map.mjs caseGraphHtml)

PL po

Removed, and where it went:

  • "so the map leaves it out" becomes "so the map shows nothing until the loop is broken": the layout refuses the whole graph, not one node (facts 1-3; M2), and the GM learns what to do (O7)
  • "a circle of inputs" becomes "a loop of inputs", the everyday word (T29)
  • PL: „leży na kole wejść, więc mapa go nie rysuje” becomes „jest w pętli wejść, więc mapa nic nie pokaże, dopóki nie przerwiesz tej pętli”: the layout draws no node at all while a loop stands (facts 1-3; M2), the GM reads what to do (O7), „pętla” the everyday word (T29)

  • PL: „Key Remnanty w sprawie: {ref}, więcej niż ...” becomes „Sprawa ma więcej Key Remnantów ({ref}), niż przygotowuje rozdział”: no label-colon-value in the sentence (X7, X12); the genitive after „więcej” holds for every count, so {ref} needs no plural family (PL7)

  • FIX: R1157: "has {ref} Key Remnants, more than" becomes "has more Key Remnants ({ref}) than", as the Polish: no count before the noun, so the census of counts (E66-15) needs no family and the value holds for every count.

  • REV: en: added "who does not hold one yet": a GM diagnostics note has no word limit, and a participant who holds a copy gets none (fact 0, M4); the earlier "left out for length" no longer holds.
